LINE 1198: function getReportTwoBlockHeightScore(
LINE 1199:   block: StudioBlock,
LINE 1200:   previewCase: ReturnType<typeof getPreviewCase>,
LINE 1201: ) {
LINE 1202:   if (block.kind === "hero-title") return 32;
LINE 1203:   if (block.kind === "meta-strip") return 28;
LINE 1204:   if (block.kind === "closing-note") return 34;
LINE 1205:   if (block.kind === "highlight-note") return 34;
LINE 1206:   if (block.kind === "plain-text") return 26;
LINE 1207: 
LINE 1208:   if (block.kind === "dynamic-fields") {
LINE 1209:     const fields = getDynamicFieldsForBlock(block, previewCase);
LINE 1210:     const visibleFields = fields.filter((f) => f.visible !== false);
LINE 1211:     const count = Math.max(visibleFields.length, 1);
LINE 1212:     return 24 + Math.ceil(count / 2) * 10.5;
LINE 1213:   }
LINE 1214: 
LINE 1215:   if (block.kind === "section-text" || block.kind === "multi-paragraph") {
LINE 1216:     const contentLength = cleanText(block.content).length;
LINE 1217:     const lineCount = Math.max(
LINE 1218:       cleanText(block.content).split("\n").length,
LINE 1219:       Math.ceil(contentLength / 80),
LINE 1220:       1,
LINE 1221:     );
LINE 1222: 
LINE 1223:     return 24 + lineCount * 5.8;
LINE 1224:   }
LINE 1225: 
LINE 1226:   if (block.kind === "bullet-list") {
LINE 1227:     const count = Math.max(
LINE 1228:       cleanText(block.content)
LINE 1229:         .split("\n")
LINE 1230:         .filter(Boolean).length,
LINE 1231:       1,
LINE 1232:     );
LINE 1233: 
LINE 1234:     return 24 + count * 7;
LINE 1235:   }
LINE 1236: 
LINE 1237:   if (block.kind === "signature-grid") return 34;
LINE 1238: 
LINE 1239:   if (block.kind === "report-one-table") {
LINE 1240:     const rowCount = Math.max(block.rows?.length || 0, 1);
LINE 1241:     return 28 + rowCount * 11;
LINE 1242:   }
LINE 1243: 
LINE 1244:   if (block.kind === "evidence-gallery") {
LINE 1245:     const perPage = getEvidencePerPageFromBlock(block);
LINE 1246:     const shown = Math.min(perPage, Math.max(previewCase.evidences.length, 1));
LINE 1247: 
LINE 1248:     if (block.evidenceLayout === "ONE_PER_PAGE") return 98;
LINE 1249:     if (block.evidenceLayout === "GRID_2X2") return 96;
LINE 1250:     if (block.evidenceLayout === "ATTACHMENT_LIST") return 24 + shown * 8;
LINE 1251: 
LINE 1252:     return 72;
LINE 1253:   }
LINE 1254: 
LINE 1255:   return 36;
LINE 1256: }

LINE 3512:     if (!selectedBlock || !activePage) return;

LINE 3523:

LINE 3525:     const [block] = nextBlocks.splice(index, 1);
LINE 3526:     nextBlocks.splice(targetIndex, 0, block);

LINE 3529:       ...page,

LINE 1307: function buildReportTwoRuntimeTemplate(

LINE 1309:   previewCase: ReturnType<typeof getPreviewCase>,
LINE 1310: ): StudioTemplate {
LINE 1311:   const runtimePages: StudioPage[] = [];
LINE 1312: 
LINE 1313:   template.pages.forEach((sourcePage) => {
LINE 1314:     let pageNumber = 1;
LINE 1315:     let usedScore = 0;
LINE 1316: 
LINE 1317:     let currentPage: StudioPage = {
LINE 1318:       ...sourcePage,
LINE 1319:       blocks: [],
LINE 1320:     };
LINE 1321: 
LINE 1322:     function pushCurrentPage() {
LINE 1323:       runtimePages.push({
LINE 1324:         ...currentPage,
LINE 1325:         blocks: currentPage.blocks,
LINE 1326:       });
LINE 1327:     }
LINE 1328: 
LINE 1329:     function startNextPage() {
LINE 1330:       pageNumber += 1;
LINE 1331:       usedScore = 0;
LINE 1332:       currentPage = makeReportTwoContinuationPage(sourcePage, pageNumber);
LINE 1333:     }
LINE 1334: 
LINE 1335:     sourcePage.blocks.forEach((originalBlock) => {
LINE 1336:       const block = normalizeReportTwoBlockForRuntime(originalBlock, previewCase);
LINE 1337:       const blockScore = getReportTwoBlockHeightScore(block, previewCase);
LINE 1338: 
LINE 1339:       if (
LINE 1340:         currentPage.blocks.length > 0 &&
LINE 1341:         usedScore + blockScore > REPORT_TWO_PAGE_SAFE_HEIGHT_SCORE
LINE 1342:       ) {
LINE 1343:         pushCurrentPage();
LINE 1344:         startNextPage();
LINE 1345:       }
LINE 1346: 
LINE 1347:       currentPage.blocks.push(block);
LINE 1348:       usedScore += blockScore;
LINE 1349: 
LINE 1350:       if (block.kind === "evidence-gallery") {
LINE 1351:         const perPage = getEvidencePerPageFromBlock(block);
LINE 1352:         const count = previewCase.evidences.length;
LINE 1353:         const pagesCount = Math.ceil(count / perPage);
LINE 1354: 
LINE 1355:         if (pagesCount > 1) {
LINE 1356:           pushCurrentPage();
LINE 1357: 
LINE 1358:           for (let index = 1; index < pagesCount; index += 1) {
LINE 1359:             const evidencePageNumber = pageNumber + index;
LINE 1360:             const evidencePage: StudioPage = {
LINE 1361:               ...sourcePage,
LINE 1362:               id: `${sourcePage.id}-${block.id}-evidence-${evidencePageNumber}`,
LINE 1363:               kind: "evidence",
LINE 1364:               title: `${block.title || "الشواهد"} - صفحة ${index + 1}`,
LINE 1365:               description: "صفحة شواهد إضافية داخل التقرير.",
LINE 1366:               sourceTemplatePageId: sourcePage.id,
LINE 1367:               reportTwoVirtualPage: true,
LINE 1368:               blocks: [
LINE 1369:                 {
LINE 1370:                   ...block,
LINE 1371:                   id: `${block.id}-evidence-${index + 1}`,
LINE 1372:                   title: `${block.title || "الشواهد"} - صفحة ${index + 1}`,
LINE 1373:                   evidenceStartIndex: index * perPage,
LINE 1374:                   evidenceLimit: perPage,
LINE 1375:                   evidenceAutoCreatePages: false,
LINE 1376:                 },
LINE 1377:               ],
LINE 1378:             };
LINE 1379: 
LINE 1380:             runtimePages.push(evidencePage);
LINE 1381:           }
LINE 1382: 
LINE 1383:           pageNumber += pagesCount - 1;
LINE 1384:           startNextPage();
LINE 1385:         }
LINE 1386:       }
LINE 1387:     });
LINE 1388: 
LINE 1389:     pushCurrentPage();
LINE 1390:   });
LINE 1391: 
LINE 1392:   return {
LINE 1393:     ...template,
LINE 1394:     pages: runtimePages.filter((page) => page.blocks.length > 0),
LINE 1395:   };
LINE 1396: }

LINE 1307: function buildReportTwoRuntimeTemplate(
LINE 1308:   template: StudioTemplate,
LINE 1309:   previewCase: ReturnType<typeof getPreviewCase>,
LINE 1310: ): StudioTemplate {

LINE 1312: 
LINE 1313:   template.pages.forEach((sourcePage) => {
LINE 1314:     let pageNumber = 1;
LINE 1315:     let usedScore = 0;
LINE 1316: 
LINE 1317:     let currentPage: StudioPage = {
LINE 1318:       ...sourcePage,
LINE 1319:       blocks: [],
LINE 1320:     };
LINE 1321: 
LINE 1322:     function pushCurrentPage() {
LINE 1323:       runtimePages.push({
LINE 1324:         ...currentPage,
LINE 1325:         blocks: currentPage.blocks,
LINE 1326:       });
LINE 1327:     }
LINE 1328:

LINE 1331:       usedScore = 0;
LINE 1332:       currentPage = makeReportTwoContinuationPage(sourcePage, pageNumber);
LINE 1333:     }
LINE 1334: 
LINE 1335:     sourcePage.blocks.forEach((originalBlock) => {
LINE 1336:       const block = normalizeReportTwoBlockForRuntime(originalBlock, previewCase);
LINE 1337:       const blockScore = getReportTwoBlockHeightScore(block, previewCase);
LINE 1338: 
LINE 1339:       if (
LINE 1340:         currentPage.blocks.length > 0 &&
LINE 1341:         usedScore + blockScore > REPORT_TWO_PAGE_SAFE_HEIGHT_SCORE
LINE 1342:       ) {
LINE 1343:         pushCurrentPage();
LINE 1344:         startNextPage();
LINE 1345:       }
LINE 1346: 
LINE 1347:       currentPage.blocks.push(block);
LINE 1348:       usedScore += blockScore;
LINE 1349: 
LINE 1350:       if (block.kind === "evidence-gallery") {
LINE 1351:         const perPage = getEvidencePerPageFromBlock(block);
LINE 1352:         const count = previewCase.evidences.length;
LINE 1353:         const pagesCount = Math.ceil(count / perPage);
LINE 1354: 
LINE 1355:         if (pagesCount > 1) {
LINE 1356:           pushCurrentPage();

LINE 1360:             const evidencePage: StudioPage = {
LINE 1361:               ...sourcePage,
LINE 1362:               id: `${sourcePage.id}-${block.id}-evidence-${evidencePageNumber}`,
LINE 1363:               kind: "evidence",
LINE 1364:               title: `${block.title || "الشواهد"} - صفحة ${index + 1}`,
LINE 1365:               description: "صفحة شواهد إضافية داخل التقرير.",
LINE 1366:               sourceTemplatePageId: sourcePage.id,
LINE 1367:               reportTwoVirtualPage: true,
LINE 1368:               blocks: [
LINE 1369:                 {
LINE 1370:                   ...block,
LINE 1371:                   id: `${block.id}-evidence-${index + 1}`,

LINE 1373:                   evidenceStartIndex: index * perPage,
LINE 1374:                   evidenceLimit: perPage,
LINE 1375:                   evidenceAutoCreatePages: false,
LINE 1376:                 },
LINE 1377:               ],
LINE 1378:             };
LINE 1379: 
LINE 1380:             runtimePages.push(evidencePage);
LINE 1381:           }
LINE 1382: 
LINE 1383:           pageNumber += pagesCount - 1;
LINE 1384:           startNextPage();
LINE 1385:         }
LINE 1386:       }
LINE 1387:     });
LINE 1388: 
LINE 1389:     pushCurrentPage();
LINE 1390:   });

LINE 3530:       blocks: nextBlocks,
LINE 3531:     }));

LINE 1487: 
LINE 1488: function reorderReportTwoPages(
LINE 1489:   pages: StudioPage[],
LINE 1490:   sourcePageId: string,
LINE 1491:   direction: "previous" | "next",
LINE 1492: ) {
LINE 1493:   const index = pages.findIndex((page) => page.id === sourcePageId);
LINE 1494: 
LINE 1495:   if (index < 0) return pages;
LINE 1496: 
LINE 1497:   const targetIndex = direction === "previous" ? index - 1 : index + 1;
LINE 1498: 
LINE 1499:   if (targetIndex < 0 || targetIndex >= pages.length) {
LINE 1500:     return pages;
LINE 1501:   }
LINE 1502: 
LINE 1503:   const nextPages = [...pages];
LINE 1504:   const [page] = nextPages.splice(index, 1);
LINE 1505:   nextPages.splice(targetIndex, 0, page);
LINE 1506: 
LINE 1507:   return nextPages;
LINE 1508: }
LINE 1509:

LINE 1511: function isReportTwoDynamicFieldsBlock(block: StudioBlock | null | undefined) {
LINE 1512:   if (!block) return false;
LINE 1513: 
LINE 1514:   const kind = cleanText(block.kind);
LINE 1515:   const smartKind = cleanText((block as any).settings?.smartBlockKind);
LINE 1516:   const title = cleanText(block.title);
LINE 1517: 
LINE 1518:   return (
LINE 1519:     kind === "dynamic-fields" ||
LINE 1520:     kind === "field-list" ||
LINE 1521:     kind === "case-meta" ||
LINE 1522:     kind === "student-summary" ||
LINE 1523:     kind === "service-summary" ||
LINE 1524:     smartKind === "dynamic-fields" ||
LINE 1525:     smartKind === "field-list" ||
LINE 1526:     title.includes("حقول") ||
LINE 1527:     title.includes("بيانات الحالة")
LINE 1528:   );
LINE 1529: }

LINE 265: 
LINE 266: function getReportTwoSavedTemplatesStorageKey(serviceSlug: string) {
LINE 267:   return `report-2:saved-runtime-templates:${serviceSlug || "general"}`;
LINE 268: }
LINE 269: 
LINE 270: function readReportTwoSavedTemplates(
LINE 271:   serviceSlug: string,
LINE 272: ): ReportTwoSavedRuntimeTemplate[] {
LINE 273:   if (typeof window === "undefined") return [];
LINE 274: 
LINE 275:   try {
LINE 276:     const raw = window.localStorage.getItem(
LINE 277:       getReportTwoSavedTemplatesStorageKey(serviceSlug),
LINE 278:     );
LINE 279: 
LINE 280:     if (!raw) return [];
LINE 281: 
LINE 282:     const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
LINE 283: 
LINE 284:     return Array.isArray(parsed) ? parsed : [];

LINE 3511:   function moveBlock(direction: "up" | "down") {
LINE 3512:     if (!selectedBlock || !activePage) return;
LINE 3513: 
LINE 3514:     const index = activePage.blocks.findIndex(
LINE 3515:       (block) => block.id === selectedBlock.id,
LINE 3516:     );
LINE 3517: 
LINE 3518:     if (index < 0) return;
LINE 3519:

LINE 3523:

LINE 3525:     const [block] = nextBlocks.splice(index, 1);
LINE 3526:     nextBlocks.splice(targetIndex, 0, block);

LINE 3529:       ...page,
LINE 3530:       blocks: nextBlocks,
LINE 3531:     }));

LINE 266: function getReportTwoSavedTemplatesStorageKey(serviceSlug: string) {
LINE 267:   return `report-2:saved-runtime-templates:${serviceSlug || "general"}`;
LINE 268: }
LINE 269: 
LINE 270: function readReportTwoSavedTemplates(
LINE 271:   serviceSlug: string,
LINE 272: ): ReportTwoSavedRuntimeTemplate[] {
LINE 273:   if (typeof window === "undefined") return [];
LINE 274: 
LINE 275:   try {
LINE 276:     const raw = window.localStorage.getItem(
LINE 277:       getReportTwoSavedTemplatesStorageKey(serviceSlug),
LINE 278:     );
LINE 279: 
LINE 280:     if (!raw) return [];
LINE 281: 
LINE 282:     const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
LINE 283: 
LINE 284:     return Array.isArray(parsed) ? parsed : [];
LINE 285:   } catch {
LINE 286:     return [];
LINE 287:   }
LINE 288: }
LINE 289: 
LINE 290: function getReportTwoSavedTemplatesStoragePrefix() {
LINE 291:   return "report-2:saved-runtime-templates:";
LINE 292: }
LINE 293: 
LINE 294: function readAllReportTwoSavedTemplates() {
LINE 295:   if (typeof window === "undefined") return [];
LINE 296: 
LINE 297:   const prefix = getReportTwoSavedTemplatesStoragePrefix();
LINE 298:   const items: ReportTwoSavedRuntimeTemplate[] = [];
LINE 299: 
LINE 300:   for (let index = 0; index < window.localStorage.length; index += 1) {
LINE 301:     const key = window.localStorage.key(index);
LINE 302: 
LINE 303:     if (!key || !key.startsWith(prefix)) continue;
LINE 304: 
LINE 305:     const serviceSlug = key.slice(prefix.length);
LINE 306:     const templates = readReportTwoSavedTemplates(serviceSlug);
LINE 307: 
LINE 308:     templates.forEach((template) => {
LINE 309:       items.push({
LINE 310:         ...template,
LINE 311:         serviceSlug: (template as any).serviceSlug || serviceSlug,
LINE 312:       } as ReportTwoSavedRuntimeTemplate);
LINE 313:     });
LINE 314:   }
LINE 315: 
LINE 316:   return items.sort((a, b) => {
LINE 317:     return String((b as any).updatedAt || (b as any).createdAt || "").localeCompare(
LINE 318:       String((a as any).updatedAt || (a as any).createdAt || ""),
LINE 319:     );
LINE 320:   });
LINE 321: }
LINE 322: 
LINE 323: function deleteReportTwoSavedTemplateFromAllServices(templateId: string) {
LINE 324:   if (typeof window === "undefined") return [];
LINE 325: 
LINE 326:   const prefix = getReportTwoSavedTemplatesStoragePrefix();
LINE 327: 
LINE 328:   for (let index = window.localStorage.length - 1; index >= 0; index -= 1) {
LINE 329:     const key = window.localStorage.key(index);
LINE 330:

LINE 332: 
LINE 333:     const serviceSlug = key.slice(prefix.length);
LINE 334:     const templates = readReportTwoSavedTemplates(serviceSlug);
